- **dexkit-decompile**: Decompile a single method (or class) via the DAD-aligned C++ pipeline and print the result. Argument is a Dalvik method descriptor (e.g. `LX/00D9;-&gt;equals(Ljava/lang/Object;)Z`) or a class descriptor (`LX/00D9;`), optionally followed by `from &lt;apk_path&gt;`. ([mobile-threat-hunter/dex-analyzer-for-llm](https://github.com/mobile-threat-hunter/dex-analyzer-for-llm/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/dexkit-decompile) · ★2)
- **dexkit-diff**: Side-by-side parity diff between androguard DAD (Python reference) and DexKit-DAD (our C++ port) for a given method descriptor. Use when investigating output divergence — confirms whether a defect is a port bug or matches DAD's behavior (in which case it's DAD-faithful and out of scope). ([mobile-threat-hunter/dex-analyzer-for-llm](https://github.com/mobile-threat-hunter/dex-analyzer-for-llm/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/dexkit-diff) · ★2)
- **dexkit-sweep**: Full-APK regression sweep across every loadable APK in test_apk/APK/. Decompiles every class, counts crashes/errors/empties, reports throughput. ([mobile-threat-hunter/dex-analyzer-for-llm](https://github.com/mobile-threat-hunter/dex-analyzer-for-llm/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/dexkit-sweep) · ★2)
- **dexkit-trace**: Reproduce, isolate, and capture a stack trace for crashes / hangs / wrong output in the DAD-aligned C++ pipeline. Use when a sweep run shows a `class_crash` entry or a method hangs indefinitely. ([mobile-threat-hunter/dex-analyzer-for-llm](https://github.com/mobile-threat-hunter/dex-analyzer-for-llm/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/dexkit-trace) · ★2)
